## Addendum 2026-10-20: mediator and instance base complete (list item 13, preparation F25)

**FaceplateInstanceMediatorData** (0x00d30007, DL.FaceplateLib), bases: HmiByRefInstanceObjectBase > HmiBase > ByRefInstanceObjectBase > HmiInstanceObjectBase > CoreObject > InstanceObject.
- AttributeSets: ICoreAttributes (stored only `IsShell`), ICoreExpandoAttributes, ICoreExpandoLinkAttributes, IAdditionalAttributes, ISoftlinkAttributes, ITextAttributes (all expando).
- Own relations: **none**. The only specific relation is `Parent` → HmiParent (from HmiBase). Everything else are the CoreObject relations: `IsInstanceOf` (1, derivedFrom) / `IsTypeOf` (*, derivesTo), `IsDerivedFrom`/`DerivesTo`, `Environment`, `Target`, `Parent`, `PartInverse`, `VisiblePartInverse`/`InvisiblePartInverse` (IsUsedPart to CoreLibraryObject), `TRefParent`/`TextualReferences`, `Expando*`, `IDRefTarget*`, `Identity`, `Comment`, `Children`.
- No relation in the metamodel targets the mediator or HmiByRefInstanceObjectBase, ByRefInstanceObjectBase, HmiInstanceObjectBase or InstanceObject.

**HmiByRefInstanceObjectBase** (0x00d21002, abstract), bases: ByRefInstanceObjectBase > HmiInstanceObjectBase > InstanceObject > CoreObject.
- AttributeSets: ICoreAttributes (stored `IsShell`; override `InstanceOf` constant=false), ICoreExpandoAttributes, ICoreExpandoLinkAttributes, IAdditionalAttributes.
- Own relations: none; only the CoreObject relations as above.

**Conclusion for F25:** the connection instance → version runs via the generic CoreObject relation `IsInstanceOf` (derivedFrom), as for typed PLC blocks (library.py: `instance_versions`). To be checked is whether ILFaceplateContainerData itself carries `IsInstanceOf` → FaceplateLibraryVersionData or whether a FaceplateInstanceMediatorData (per device and version?) sits in between. No sample so far has a typed instance or a mediator.
